The classifier derives the occlusion-level attention, which serves as spatial guidance, while the occlusion labels are encoded into a learnable embedding to produce channel-wise weights. Through joint modulation, PEMOLA elegantly introduces the occlusion priors into the position embedding, thereby improving the occlusion modelling. We further annotate the Cityscapes dataset with occlusion levels, termed Cityscapes Occlusion Labels for All Computer Vision Tasks (Cityscapes-OLAC), following the same labelling protocol as COCO-OLAC, to evaluate the cross-dataset generalisation ability of PEMOLA. Extensive experiments on COCO-OLAC and Cityscapes-OLAC demonstrate that PEMOLA consistently improves panoptic segmentation quality while introducing minimal computational overhead. These results highlight the importance of occlusion modelling, where incorporating occlusion-level attention helps deliver robust panoptic segmentation under occlusion. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/wenbo-wei/PEMOLA.

</details>


### [183] [Lossless-INR: Lossless Volumetric Implicit Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18150)
*Kaiyuan Tang,Daniel Burke,Chaoli Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Lossless-INR，基于位平面分解、八叉树分块和三元特征网格网络，实现3D科学体数据的无损神经表示与精确重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐式神经表示方法对体数据进行有损压缩，即使微小重建误差也会在渲染和下游分析中传播累积，迫切需要一种无损且紧凑的表示框架。

Method: 将体素值分解为二进制位平面，将重建转化为逐位二分类问题；结合八叉树自适应分块策略处理复杂区域，并采用三元特征网格网络参数化网格条目以实现紧凑表示。

Result: 在多种体数据集上实现零比特错误率和比特级精确重建，支持忠实渲染和下游分析，同时保持表示紧凑。

Conclusion: 本文提出的Lossless-INR框架成功实现了体数据的无损神经表示，兼顾精确性与紧凑性，为科学可视化等应用提供了可靠基础。

Abstract: Implicit neural representation (INR) methods provide continuous coordinate-to-value mappings and integrate naturally with direct volume rendering, making them attractive for representing volumetric data. However, existing INR-based approaches for volumetric data are inherently lossy, and even small reconstruction errors can propagate through rendering and downstream analysis. In this work, we explore Lossless-INR, a lossless INR framework for 3D scientific volumetric data based on bit-plane decomposition. By decomposing each voxel value into binary bit-planes, we reformulate reconstruction as per-bit binary classification, so that exact recovery reduces to predicting every bit correctly. To make this optimization tractable while keeping the representation compact, we combine an octree block-partitioning strategy that adaptively subdivides complex regions with a ternary feature-grid network whose grid entries are parameterized by a ternary set of values. Experiments on diverse volumetric datasets show that this design can achieve zero bit-error rate and bit-exact reconstruction, enabling faithful rendering and downstream analysis with a compact representation. The code is available at https://github.com/TouKaienn/Lossless-INR.

</details>


### [184] [Plenoptic Condensation: A Novel Approach to Generalized Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18151)
*Brevin Tilmon,Alex DeJournett,John Leffingwell,Scott Ackerson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Plenoptic Condensation (PCon)的新型广义场景重建方法，通过多阶段重建管道将图像转换为具有不同表示能力的场景元素，实现了高保真渲染和精确测量，在损坏车辆重建任务中精度远超现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GSR方法在表示空间变化细节（如锐利边缘和光滑反射面）方面能力有限，难以同时实现高保真渲染和精确测量，尤其缺乏对局部微小损伤的检测能力。

Method: PCon采用多阶段重建管道：首先将图像转换为低表示能力的"汤状"场景元素，然后自适应地浓缩为高表示能力的"结构化"元素，形成称为Reality Models (Relms)的场景模型，实现空间变化的表示能力。

Result: 在"Damaged Fiat"案例中，PCon对汽车引擎盖的重建精度是NeRO和RT-Splatting的两倍以上；局部损伤剖面误差仅为35微米（0.035毫米），而两种SOTA方法基本无法测量损伤。

Conclusion: PCon通过自适应表示能力分配，在真实场景重建中实现了远超现有方法的精度和细节保真度，尤其适用于需要精确测量和理解的场景。

Abstract: We present a novel Generalized Scene Reconstruction (GSR) approach called Plenoptic Condensation (PCon). PCon uses a multi-stage reconstruction pipeline, initially converting images into "soupy" scene elements with low (representational) power, then adaptively condensing the "soup" into "structured" elements of higher power capable of efficiently representing, for example, sharp edges and smooth reflective surfaces. PCon scene models called Reality Models (Relms) enable spatially varying representational power, which is essential for high-fidelity rendering, measurement, and scene understanding. We showcase several in-the-wild PCon reconstructions captured with consumer phone cameras and drones. In one case called "Damaged Fiat", PCon is benchmarked against two state-of-the-art (SOTA) GSR methods: NeRO and RT-Splatting. Referring to Figure 1 below, PCon reconstructs the car hood more than twice as accurately as the SOTA methods. But more importantly, the local damage profile error for PCon is 35 um (0.035 mm), whereas the two other SOTA methods are essentially unable to measure the damage at all. Our project website is available at https://quidient.github.io/pcon-2026.html.

</details>


### [185] [Robust Multimodal Dynamic Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18153)
*Zhe Xin,Hanzhi Chang,Penghui Huang,Yinian Mao,Guoquan Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合2D点轨迹、3D重建和语义信息的多模态动态目标分割框架，通过Transformer与特征聚类聚合模块实现静态/动态分类，并引入基于点查询的SAM后处理方法，在动态分割和静态场景重建任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光流法在动态目标分割中无法保证边界一致性，而基于3D重建的方法对重建误差高度敏感，亟需一种融合多模态线索的鲁棒分割方法。

Method: 设计结合Transformer与特征聚类聚合模块的网络，对多模态特征轨迹进行静态/动态分类；提出点查询式SAM后处理方法以处理单掩码中的多目标。

Result: 在动态目标分割和静态场景重建任务上均达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 融合多模态线索（2D点轨迹、3D重建、语义信息）能有效克服单一模态的局限性，实现精确且完整的动态物体分割。

Abstract: Dynamic object segmentation plays a critical role in many visual applications such as static scene reconstruction from dynamic videos. However, existing optical flow-based methods fail to ensure consistent static/dynamic segmentation along object boundaries, while 3D reconstruction-based approaches are highly sensitive to reconstruction errors. To address these limitations, we present a dynamic object segmentation framework that can generate both precise and complete dynamic masks by integrating multimodal cues including 2D point tracks, 3D reconstruction, and semantic information. We design a network combining Transformer architectures with feature clustering aggregation modules to perform static/dynamic classification of multimodal feature trajectories. It enables the model to adaptively determine which type of feature should dominate based on the characteristics of each scene, while also mitigating the impact of feature degradation. Additionally, we introduce a novel point-query-based SAM post-processing method capable of handling multiple objects within a single mask. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance in both dynamic object segmentation and static scene reconstruction tasks.

</details>


### [186] [Certified Training for Convolutional Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18195)
*Benedikt Brückner,Alessio Lomuscio*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于卷积扰动高效编码的认证训练方法，能在保证模型对运动模糊等扰动鲁棒性的同时提供形式化安全保证，在CIFAR10上对运动模糊实现了超过80%的鲁棒准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉模型易受运动模糊等扰动影响，现有方法（数据增强、对抗训练）缺乏形式化安全保证，难以识别和缓解隐藏的漏洞，阻碍了在关键应用中的部署。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的认证训练（Certified Training）方法，利用对卷积扰动的高效编码来训练可证明鲁棒的模型。

Result: 在CIFAR10数据集上，该方法对合理强度的运动模糊实现了超过80%的鲁棒准确率，同时保持了可比的常规准确率，显著优于对抗训练。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在提供形式化安全保证的同时有效提升模型对运动模糊等扰动的鲁棒性，为视觉模型在关键应用中的部署提供了更可靠的保障。

Abstract: Vision models have been found to be susceptible to perturbations such as motion blur induced at runtime by a shaking camera. This impedes their deployment in critical applications since phenomena such as slightly blurred vision might lead to failures, for example an object detector missing objects. While methods such as data augmentation or Adversarial Training can improve empirical robustness, they lack formal safety guarantees, making it difficult to identify and mitigate hidden vulnerabilities. We introduce a novel Certified Training approach that leverages an efficient encoding of convolutional perturbations to train provably robust models. Our method significantly outperforms Adversarial Training, achieving, for example, over 80% robust accuracy against motion blur of reasonable intensity on CIFAR10 while maintaining comparable standard accuracy.

</details>


### [187] [HOMIE: Human-object Centric Video Personalization via Multimodal Intelligent Enchancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18217)
*Yiyang Cai,Nan Chen,Rongchang Xie,Junwen Pan,Chunyang Jiang,Cheng Chen,Wen Zhou,Zhenbang Sun,Wei Xue,Wenhan Luo,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HOMIE框架，通过改进MLLM集成策略和全局多模态引导，统一解决跨主体和主体内参考设置下的人-物交互视频个性化生成问题，实现高主体保真度与准确交互模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在主体保真度与交互模式准确性之间难以平衡，且缺乏理解主体内参考隐式对应关系的机制。

Method: 1) 改进MLLM集成策略提取参考级关系知识；2) 在自注意力中引入全局多模态引导对齐语义特征与VAE标记；3) 提出模态参考嵌入区分不同来源的标记并关联主体内参考图像标记。

Result: 在多种人-物交互视频个性化任务上达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: HOMIE框架有效统一了跨主体和主体内输入设置，兼顾了主体保真度与交互准确性，且无需昂贵的重新对齐。

Abstract: Human-object centric video personalization (HOCVP) is a core task within subject-driven video generation. However, existing methods suffer from two key limitations. First, most approaches focusing on inter-subject personalization still struggle to strike a balance between high subject fidelity and accurate interaction patterns between humans and diverse objects, especially when objects represent abstract concepts such as logos. Second, while intra-subject references (e.g., OCR maps, multi-view inputs) are expected to enhance subject fidelity, most existing works lack mechanisms to understand such latent correspondence. To address both challenges, we propose HOMIE, an HOCVP framework that tackles both inter- and intra-subject input settings in a unified manner. Compared to previous approaches, HOMIE proposes a better MLLM integration strategy to extract knowledge of reference-level relationships without compromising the controllability of text encoders or incurring costly re-alignment. Specifically, we introduce global multimodal guidance within self-attention to better align MLLM-derived semantic features with VAE tokens. Furthermore, we propose modality-reference embedding to differentiate tokens from MLLM features and VAE tokens and associate intra-subject reference image tokens. Extensive experiments validate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance across various HOCVP tasks. Project Page: https://yiyangcai.github.io/homie-page.github.io/

</details>


### [188] [GigaPath-Flash and GigaTIME-Flash: Efficient Pathology Foundation Models for Whole-Slide and Tumor Microenvironment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18218)
*Naoto Usuyama,Jeya Maria Jose Valanarasu,Sicong Yao,Hanwen Xu,Jaspreet Bagga,Guanghui Qin,Robert E. Kramer,Cliff Wong,Soohee Lee,Hao Qiu,Theodore Zhengde Zhao,Racheli Ben Shimol,Angela Crabtree,Kevin Matlock,Eduardo Alejandro Lozano Garcia,Naiteek Sangani,Alberto Santamaria-Pang,Jason Entenmann,Alexandra Q. Bartlett,Bill J. Wright,Bernard A. Fox,Brian Piening,Sheng Zhang,Sheng Wang,Tristan Naumann,Carlo Bifulco,Hoifung Poon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GigaPath-Flash和GigaTIME-Flash两个高效病理学基础模型，通过知识蒸馏将十亿参数模型压缩至千万参数级别，保留97%性能的同时减少50倍计算量，并以Apache-2.0开源协议发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有病理学基础模型多局限于图像块级别、许可证限制严格且计算成本高昂，阻碍了大规模全切片临床和研究应用。

Method: GigaPath-Flash采用22M参数的ViT-S瓦片编码器+21M参数的LongNet全切片编码器架构，通过知识蒸馏从十亿参数GigaPath(ViT-g)教师模型训练得到紧凑编码器；GigaTIME-Flash扩展该骨干网络用于H&E图像预测肿瘤免疫微环境。

Result: GigaPath-Flash以50倍更少计算量保留GigaPath 97%的平均切片级性能；GigaTIME-Flash超越原CNN版GigaTIME，运行快6倍、GPU内存减少8倍。

Conclusion: 开源发布全套模型及权重，为计算病理学、免疫肿瘤学和精准健康提供易于获取的基础组件。

Abstract: Foundation models have emerged as a driving force in computational pathology, with the potential to transform cancer diagnosis, prognosis, and treatment selection by learning transferable representations from large-scale histopathology data. A growing landscape of pathology foundation models now spans diverse data sources, architectures, and downstream applications. However, most pretrained models operate only at the image-tile level, use restrictive licenses, and remain computationally expensive, limiting large-scale slide-level clinical and research use.
  Here, we introduce GigaPath-Flash and GigaTIME-Flash, efficient models for whole-slide pathology AI and spatial proteomics prediction. GigaPath-Flash combines a 22M-parameter ViT-S tile encoder with a 21M-parameter LongNet slide encoder, both pretrained on large-scale real-world histopathology data. Its compact tile encoder is distilled from the billion-parameter GigaPath (ViT-g) teacher and shared by both models. GigaPath-Flash retains 97% of GigaPath's average slide-level performance with 50x less compute. GigaTIME-Flash extends this backbone to predict the tumor immune microenvironment directly from routine H&E images. It surpasses the original CNN-based GigaTIME in prediction quality while running 6x faster and using 8x less GPU memory.
  Together with GigaPath and GigaTIME, these models form an open-weight, Apache-2.0-licensed family pretrained on large-scale real-world clinical data. By releasing all models and weights, we provide accessible building blocks for computational pathology, immuno-oncology, and precision health.

</details>


### [189] [FlowMimic: Mask-free Visual Editing and Generation with Pixel-pair Warped Flow Field for Online Video Editing Data Generation and Modality Mimicry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18227)
*Dingyun Zhang,Lixue Gong,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探索在统一模型中融合视频和图像的生成与编辑能力，提出像素对时间扭曲流场实现从图像编辑到视频编辑的实时转换，并通过模态模仿损失和感知相关任务使模型内化编辑能力，无需外部辅助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视频编辑数据收集依赖繁琐的人工标注、I2V模型合成及VLM质量筛选，流程耗时且任务扩展性有限，导致视频编辑任务多样性远不及图像编辑模型。

Method: (1) 提出像素对时间扭曲流场，从图像编辑样本实时生成视频编辑样本；(2) 将图像视为视频的特殊形式，设计模态模仿生成损失和编辑损失来对齐两种模态的输出分布；(3) 引入感知相关任务（如指代分割）及编辑区域感知的潜在层和注意力层损失，使模型内化区域定位与编辑能力。

Result: 在多种视频编辑任务上证明，模型仅使用此类数据即可学习视频编辑，无需依赖外部辅助（如额外MLLM微调或显式掩码输入）。

Conclusion: 通过数据生成方法、模态对齐损失和感知任务的设计，实现了视频与图像编辑能力的统一，且模型能够内化编辑区域定位能力，提升了任务扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: In line with the prevailing direction of vision research, we explore the integration of both generation and editing capabilities for video and image modalities within a single model. Current approaches to collecting video editing data typically depend on labour-intensive, time-consuming curated procedures--involving object mask annotation, the use of error-introducing pair synthesis via I2V model and ControlNet-like guidance, and VLM-based quality filtering or refinement--and demonstrate limited task scalability. As a result, the diversity of editing tasks remains substantially narrower than that available for image editing models. We develop a pixel-pair temporal warped flow field that can directly generate corresponding video editing samples in real time from image editing samples, and we demonstrate across multiple levels of video editing tasks that a model can learn video editing using only such data. We regard the image modality as a particular form of the video modality. Accordingly, we design a modality mimic generation loss and a modality mimic editing loss to relatively align the capabilities--and thereby the output distributions--of the two modalities through mutual imitation. Moreover, language-based visual editing entails the comprehension of the editing instruction and the reference visual content, the localization of the region corresponding to that instruction within the reference visual contents, and the modification of that region alone. Existing approaches predominantly rely on external aids, such as fine-tuning an additional MLLM or explicitly supplying a mask sequence as auxiliary input during inference. In contrast, we aspire for the model to internalize this capability. To that end, we introduce sense-related tasks--for instance, referring expression segmentation--along with corresponding editing-region-aware latent-level loss and attention-level loss.

</details>


### [190] [Simple Domain Generalization for Strong Pixel-Level Image Tampering Detection in Modern VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18230)
*Yi Tang,Xinyi Shang,Jiacheng Cui,Sondos Mahmoud Bsharat,Jiacheng Liu,Xiaohan Zhao,Tran Dinh Tien,Ahmed Elhagry,Salwa K. Al Khatib,Tianjun Yao,Yonina C. Eldar,Jing-Hao Xue,Hao Li,Salman Khan,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向现代视觉语言模型（如ChatGPT、Gemini等）生成图像的像素级篡改检测域泛化训练框架，通过平衡小批量采样和延迟注入策略，在跨模型和分布外场景下显著超越此前最优方法PIXAR，平均gIoU和cIoU分别相对提升26.1%和26.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代VLM大幅提升了图像生成与编辑能力，使得像素级图像篡改检测在跨模型和分布外场景下变得日益重要且极具挑战。现有方法在新出现的VLM生成分布上泛化能力不足。

Method: 提出域泛化训练框架，包含两个策略：(1) 平衡小批量采样，在每批次中策略性地采样篡改和真实图像，防止优化偏置；(2) 延迟注入策略，先在大量基础数据上训练至稳定收敛，再引入少量新兴VLM分布的支撑数据，提升适应性同时避免过拟合。

Result: 在GPT-Images-2.0、Gemini-3.1、FLUX.2、Seedream 4.5等分布外VLM上，平均gIoU和cIoU相比PIXAR分别相对提升26.1%和26.8%。

Conclusion: 该框架概念简单但效果显著，为现代VLM生成的图像篡改检测提供了一种强有效的域泛化方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Modern vision-language models (VLMs) have significantly improved image generation and editing capabilities, making pixel-level image tampering detection increasingly important yet challenging under cross-model and out-of-distribution shifts. This work studies domain generalization for pixel-level image tampering detection in modern VLMs like ChatGPT, Gemini, Qwen-Image, etc., aiming to learn tampering localization models that remain robust across diverse VLM-generated manipulation distributions. We propose a simple yet effective domain-generalized training framework built on two practical strategies. First, we introduce a balanced minibatch sampling scheme that strategically samples tampered and real images in each minibatch, preventing biased optimization toward either manipulated artifacts or clean-image priors and avoiding training collapse, ensuring that each optimization step receives proper sampled gradient signals. Second, we adopt a simple late-injection strategy, where the detector is first trained on large-scale base data until stable convergence, and then exposed to a small amount of newly selected supporting data from emerging VLM distributions, improving adaptability without overfitting to limited new domains. Together, these components provide a simple yet strong recipe for improving pixel-level tampering localization and OOD robustness across modern VLMs. Despite the conceptual simplicity, our framework outperforms the prior state-of-the-art PIXAR by a large margin of 26.1% and 26.8% relative improvement in average gIoU and cIoU, respectively, across OOD VLMs of GPT-Images-2.0, Gemini-3.1, FLUX.2, and Seedream 4.5. Our code is available at https://github.com/VILA-Lab/PIXAR-DG

</details>


### [191] [The Many Senses of Visual Similarity: A Text-Prompted Image Perceptual Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18237)
*Sheng-Yu Wang,Yotam Nitzan,Aaron Hertzmann,Jun-Yan Zhu,Eli Shechtman,Alexei A. Efros,Richard Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TPIPS，一种基于文本提示的多方面视觉相似性度量方法，通过大规模三元组数据集和VLM微调，在多项任务中超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有感知相似性度量将视觉相似性简化为单一标量，无法根据特定方面（如形状或颜色）进行条件化判断，而人类视觉相似性判断是上下文相关的。

Method: 构建大规模图像三元组数据集，每个三元组包含多个自由形式的语义方面标注；在此基础上微调视觉语言模型（VLM），得到Text-Prompted Image Perceptual Similarity (TPIPS)度量，可通过文本提示控制相似性判断的侧重点。

Result: 基准测试显示前沿VLM与人工标注存在显著差距；TPIPS与人类感知更一致，能可靠泛化到训练分布之外；在文本引导检索、组合搜索和生成模型细粒度评估中展现新能力。

Conclusion: TPIPS通过文本提示实现了多方面视觉相似性的灵活度量，填补了现有方法的空白，并解锁了多项实际应用的新功能。

Abstract: Human visual similarity judgments are context-dependent. For example, two images may be similar in shape but distinct in color. Existing perceptual similarity metrics, however, collapse these nuances into a single scalar value, offering no mechanism to condition on specific aspects. To bridge this gap, we introduce a large-scale dataset of human similarity judgments over image triplets, where each triplet is annotated across multiple, free-form semantic aspects of similarity. Benchmarking a broad range of frontier vision-language models (VLMs) reveals a considerable performance gap compared to human annotators' consensus. Leveraging our data, we fine-tune a VLM to produce our Text-Prompted Image Perceptual Similarity (TPIPS) metric, capturing multiple senses of visual similarity depending on the specified text prompt. We demonstrate that TPIPS aligns more closely with human perception and generalizes reliably beyond the training distribution. Finally, we show that TPIPS unlocks new capabilities in text-guided retrieval, compositional search, and the fine-grained evaluation of generative models. Our code, data, and trained models are at https://peterwang512.github.io/TPIPS

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [192] [Multi-level context Modeling for consistent expert selection in Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16427)
*Shuhan Huang,Naifan Zhang,Yuanbo Tang,Yang Li,Wai Kin Victor Chan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MCF-MOE框架，通过跨层语义聚合和局部标记交互构建上下文感知表示，以解决MoE模型中路由决策不稳定和语义不一致的问题，实验证明该方法能提升路由一致性和下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE路由机制通常基于浅层或孤立的token表示来选择专家，导致路由决策不稳定且跨层语义不一致。作者识别出"上下文不完整性"是限制专家有效专业化的关键瓶颈。

Method: 提出多层级上下文融合的MoE框架(MCF-MOE)，通过整合跨层语义聚合信号和局部token级交互来构建上下文感知的表示，从而实现更信息丰富且一致的专家选择。

Result: 在语言建模和理解基准测试上，MCF-MOE相比强MoE基线方法，在路由一致性和下游性能上均有一致提升。

Conclusion: 证明了上下文完整性在专家路由中的重要性，为提升MoE模型的专家专业化提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) enables efficient scaling of Transformer models by routing tokens to a small subset of experts. However, existing routers typically condition expert selection on shallow or isolated token representations, which often produce unstable and semantically inconsistent routing decisions across layers. In this work, we revisit expert selection from a representation perspective and identify context incompleteness as a key bottleneck limiting effective expert specialization. To address this issue, we propose Multi-level Context Fusion MOE (MCF-MOE), a framework that constructs context-aware representations by integrating complementary signals from cross-layer semantic aggregation and local token-level interactions, enabling more informative and consistent expert selection. Experiments on language modeling and understanding benchmarks demonstrate that MCF-MOE consistently improves routing consistency and downstream performance over strong MoE baselines, highlighting the importance of contextual completeness in expert routing. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/MCFMOE.

</details>


### [193] [RIMS: Preference Optimization via Smoothed Multi-pair Aggregation for Small-Scale LLM Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16431)
*Pei Tian,Zihan Dong,Tianci Liu,Linjun Zhang,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出RIMS框架，通过三阶段偏好优化（合成CoT数据生成、可微软聚合机制、偏好优化）提升小语言模型在检索增强生成中对噪声证据的鲁棒性，在多跳QA基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型在资源受限场景下进行RAG时，对噪声或虚假检索证据高度敏感。现有基于偏好的方法（如RoseRAG）只选最难的单个偏好对（硬argmin/argmax），丢弃剩余信号；或将多个偏好对视为独立二元比较，数据利用率低。

Method: (1) 通过目标SLM自身的拒绝采样生成合成链式思维偏好数据，不依赖专有模型；(2) 提出可微软聚合机制，用平滑算子替代硬选择，保留所有偏好对的梯度信号同时保持间隔感知选择的判别结构；(3) 将平滑后的目标应用于多种对齐算法进行偏好优化。理论上证明了平滑近似的误差可控性及软聚合相比硬选择与oracle目标的梯度对齐更紧。

Result: 在四个多跳问答基准上，RIMS在多个SLM骨干下均优于SOTA基线，在噪声检索条件下在Exact Match和F1上取得一致提升。

Conclusion: RIMS通过软聚合充分利用所有偏好对信号，有效提升了小模型在噪声RAG场景下的鲁棒性和性能。

Abstract: Small-scale language models (SLMs) are attractive for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) in resource-constrained settings, but their limited capacity makes them highly sensitive to noisy or spurious retrieved evidence. Existing preference-based methods such as RoseRAG select only the hardest single preference pair via hard argmin/argmax, discarding the remaining signal; others treat multiple pairs as independent binary comparisons, resulting in low data utilization. We propose RIMS, a three-stage preference optimization framework comprising (1) synthetic chain-of-thought preference data generation via rejection sampling using the target SLM itself without relying on proprietary models, (2) a differentiable soft aggregation mechanism that replaces hard selection with a smooth operator, preserving gradient signal from all preference pairs while retaining the discriminative structure of margin-aware selection, and (3) preference optimization with the smoothed objective applied to multiple alignment algorithms. We theoretically show that the smoothed approximation admits a controllable error bound and that smooth aggregation yields provably tighter gradient alignment to the oracle objective than hard selection. Experiments on four multi-hop question answering benchmarks show that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across multiple SLM backbones, achieving consistent gains in Exact Match and F1 under noisy retrieval conditions. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/tptrix29/RIMS.

</details>


### [194] [Committed Before Reasoning: Behavioral Reproduction and Preliminary Activation-Level Evidence of Answer Pre-Commitment in an Open-Weight LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16451)
*Heejin Jo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究揭示聊天模型存在“先承诺后推理”的问题：模型先在内部偏向错误答案（如“步行”），再生成合理化推理，即使正确答案是“开车”。通过Qwen3-8B实验，85-100%的采样轨迹错误承诺，且激活层级证据显示模型在输出答案前已偏向错误选项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前聊天模型在推理时可能存在“先决定答案，再找理由”的反向推理现象，而非真正的逻辑推导。这种“承诺偏差”会损害模型的可靠性和可解释性，需要严格验证其存在性及其机制。

Method: 设计最小化测试场景（洗车问题），在Qwen3-8B上跨5种系统提示条件进行210次采样和贪婪解码实验。通过预训练的无训练激活预言机（activation oracle）探测隐藏状态，在答案输出前的隐层位置检测“walk”和“drive”的激活强度。

Result: 行为层面：85-100%的采样轨迹错误承诺“步行”，贪婪解码100%错误，4096 token思考预算也无法修复。激活层面：最终回答“步行”的轨迹在输出前“walk”读出率68%；更关键的是，最终回答“开车”的轨迹中5/6在承诺前也读出了“walk”偏向。控制实验排除词汇偏差和文本恢复解释。

Conclusion: 初步证据表明模型在推理前就已形成错误承诺，且这种偏差存在于激活层级。方法论上强调：必须使用正控制（positive control）来验证预言机的有效性，否则负结果不可解释。

Abstract: Chat models sometimes commit to an answer and then produce reasoning that justifies it rather than deriving it -- even when the answer contradicts a task premise. We study a minimal probe: "I want to wash my car. The car wash is 100 meters away. Should I walk or drive?" Only drive works (the car must be at the car wash), yet models overwhelmingly recommend walking. (1) Behavioral reproduction: on Qwen3-8B across five system-prompt conditions (210 rollouts), the wrong commitment occurs in 85-100% of sampled rollouts per condition and 100% of greedy rollouts, in both thinking and non-thinking modes; a 4,096-token thinking budget does not repair it. (2) Preliminary activation-level evidence: probing hidden states with a pretrained, training-free activation oracle (no task-specific probe training) at positions before the answer text is emitted, "walk" read-outs exceed a neutral-context baseline (68% vs. 17%; walk-committing rollouts p=.005, drive-committing rollouts p=.005, Fisher exact) -- notably, rollouts that eventually answer drive also read as walk-leaning before commitment (5/6). The oracle's default on unrelated content is "drive" (83%), so the read-outs are not lexical bias; stratifying by literal walk/drive occurrence shows they are not text recovery either (spans containing "drive" still read out walk; in balanced lexical fields, per-rollout walk-majorities beat a per-prompt neutral baseline 15/22 vs. 1/8, p=.01; drive-committing rollouts 6/6, p=.002). Samples are small and the within-rollout positional gradient is not significant (p=.34); we frame these results as preliminary. (3) Methodological: with fixed oracle, activations, and positions, question wording alone moves a positive control from 2/16 (open question) to 11/16 (closed); negative oracle results are uninterpretable without per-wording positive controls.

</details>


### [195] [Encoding EEG Signals to Examine Human-Like Next-Word Prediction Behaviour in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16549)
*Boi Mai Quach,Binh T. Nguyen,Cathal Gurrin,Graham Healy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过EEG记录的ERP信号，比较人类和语言模型在阅读理解中的认知一致性，发现仅surprisal指标与语言处理ERP相关，且更大规模的语言模型并不必然更接近人类认知处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型的高预测准确率是否意味着其能够捕捉人类阅读理解中的认知信号，验证模型与人类认知处理的一致性。

Method: 基于top-1预测和surprisal两种信息度量生成人类和语言模型的回归量，通过EEG记录的ERP信号预测不同认知处理阶段，分析模型与人类认知的吻合程度。

Result: 仅surprisal与语言处理ERP存在潜在相关性，尤其对于语义含量高的开放类词汇；模型参数和计算规模的扩大并不持续提升与人类认知处理的趋同性。

Conclusion: 单纯提升模型规模和预测准确率并不能保证模型更贴近人类的语言认知处理，需要更细粒度的认知评估方法。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) are trained to excel at predicting the next word in the sequence given prior context, and humans also share this predictability in reading comprehension. Neuroscience research reveals that next-word predictability influences brain response, as recorded at millisecond resolution using electroencephalography (EEG). While our evidence indicates that advanced LMs achieve accuracies closely aligned with human performance at the next-word prediction task, this raises the question: Does higher prediction accuracy necessarily mean that these models adequately capture the cognitive signals associated with human reading comprehension? Here, we generate regressors for both humans and LMs based on two information measures, including top-1 prediction and surprisal, to predict event-related potential (ERP) elicited from EEG recordings which reflect different stages of cognitive processing during reading. We argue that modelling ERP patterns offers fine-grained analysis of the cognitive plausibility of various LMs during reading. Our results indicate that only surprisal potentially correlates with language-processing ERPs, especially for open-class words with high semantic content. Moreover, our findings challenge the assumption that scaling LMs with increased parameters and computational budgets will consistently lead to improved convergence with human-like linguistic processing.

</details>


### [196] [NOWJ@COLIEE 2026: Adaptive Pipelines for Legal Retrieval and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16603)
*Thuong-Hieu Ngo,Hoang-Trung Nguyen,Huu-Dong Nguyen,Xuan-Bach Le,Le-Dung Nguyen,Quang-Thanh Tran,Ha-Thanh Nguyen,Thi-Hai-Yen Vuong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了NOWJ团队在COLIEE 2026竞赛全部五个任务中的方法与成果，涵盖法律案例检索、法律案例蕴含、成文法检索与蕴含、法律文本蕴含以及法律判决预测，各任务分别采用多阶段流水线、检索增强生成、动态路由等创新方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参加COLIEE 2026竞赛，针对法律信息处理中的多项任务提出有效解决方案，提升法律文本检索、推理与预测性能。

Method: Task1采用四阶段流水线（候选过滤+密集检索+交叉编码重排序+自适应截断预测）；Task2结合BM25过滤、T5重排序与LLM蕴含验证的共识集成；Task3采用检索增强生成框架（密集检索+注意力重排序+少样本LLM推理）；Task4提出动态路由流水线（根据查询难度分配至平衡少样本求解器或结构化零样本思维链求解器）；Pilot Task结合层次化Transformer与CRF层、论点关系挖掘及概率论证图推理。

Result: 参与了COLIEE 2026全部五项任务，各任务均取得了相应结果，具体性能指标未在摘要中详述。

Conclusion: NOWJ团队针对法律领域多项任务设计了定制化的流水线方案，验证了多阶段检索、LLM推理、动态路由等策略在法律文本处理中的有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents the methodologies and results of the NOWJ team's participation across all five tasks of the COLIEE 2026 competition. For Task 1 (Legal Case Retrieval), we propose a four-stage pipeline comprising candidate filtering, dense retrieval with complementary embedding models, cross-encoder reranking via fine-tuned generative rerankers and MLP-based pairwise classification, and adaptive per-query cutoff prediction. For Task 2 (Legal Case Entailment), we combine BM25 filtering, T5-based reranking, and LLM-based entailment verification with consensus ensemble. For Task 3 (Statute Law Retrieval and Entailment), we adopt a retrieval-augmented generation framework with dense retrieval, attention-based reranking, and few-shot-prompted LLM reasoning. For Task 4 (Legal Textual Entailment), we introduce a dynamic routing pipeline that classifies query difficulty and dispatches cases to either a balanced few-shot solver or a structured zero-shot chain-of-thought solver. For the Pilot Task (Legal Judgment Prediction), we combine hierarchical transformers with CRF layers, argument relation mining, and probabilistic argumentation graph reasoning.

</details>


### [197] [From Memory to Skills: Evidence-Grounded Co-Evolution Governance for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16621)
*Bo Tang,Yang Zhang,Guomian Zhuang,Wenqiang Wei,Gaoyang Zheng,Lindong Xie,Yanchao Tan,Feiyu Xiong,Qingyu Yang,Edward Chung,Zhiyu li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MSCE框架，让LLM智能体将经验转化为可执行技能，无需训练即可实现记忆与技能的协同进化，在实验中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统将历史轨迹视为被动上下文，未能将其转化为智能体可执行的能力，限制了长期任务中的性能提升。

Method: 提出MSCE框架，包含(1)将经验组织为步骤轨迹、可重用过程策略和声明式环境认知；(2)将证据支持的L2策略结晶为可调用技能；(3)引入反思加权价值反向传播，利用密集自我反思校准轨迹价值。

Result: 在EvoAgentBench和LoCoMo基准上显著优于现有技能增强和记忆驱动方法，展现出强跨域迁移能力和终身进化能力。

Conclusion: MSCE通过无训练的记忆-技能协同进化机制，有效将经验转化为可执行能力，为长期任务智能体提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Existing memory systems for long-horizon LLM agents often retrieve prior traces as passive context rather than converting them into executable capabilities. In this paper, we propose MSCE, a training-free Memory--Skill Co-Evolution framework that organizes agent experience into grounded step traces, reusable procedural policies, and declarative environmental cognition. MSCE crystallizes evidence-backed L2 policies with positive estimated gain into callable skills that retain evidence links, applicability boundaries, decision guidance, verification rules, and reliability estimates. It further introduces reflection-weighted value backfilling, which propagates sparse terminal feedback through dense local self-reflections to produce evidence-calibrated trace values for governing memory and skill evolution. Experiments on EvoAgentBench and LoCoMo demonstrate that MSCE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art skill-augmented and memory-driven agent baselines, exhibiting strong cross-domain transferability and lifelong-evolution capabilities.

</details>


### [198] [OpenLanguageModel: Readable and Composable Small-Language-Model Pretraining for Education and Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16669)
*Tavish Mankash,Vardhaman Kalloli,Keshava Prasad,Deepan Muthirayan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OLM是一个开源PyTorch库，用于构建和预训练小型语言模型，特点是代码架构透明、可读性强，支持从教学到完整训练的全流程，包含27种预设模型和良好可用性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言模型库（如Hugging Face Transformers）往往将模型架构封装得过于复杂，使得研究者难以理解和修改模型内部结构。OLM旨在提供一个代码即架构、透明可读的开源库，降低小型语言模型研究和教学的门槛。

Method: OLM采用模块化设计，核心组件包括Block、Residual、Repeat、Parallel等描述模型连接方式的模块，使模型代码直接反映架构图。它连接了可读模型层与分词器、数据集、优化、混合精度、回调、检查点等训练基础设施，并支持CPU、单GPU和单节点多GPU执行。通过AutoTrainer实现自动硬件配置。

Result: 验证结果显示：与独立参考实现高度一致；348M参数模型在四GPU上达到90.6%的弱扩展效率；支持紧凑的架构编辑；早期可用性测试结果积极。包含9个常见模型家族的27个预设配置，采用MIT许可证发布。

Conclusion: OLM成功构建了一个透明、可读且功能完备的小型语言模型研究和教学平台，通过代码即架构的设计理念，使模型代码直接反映架构图，验证了其在教学、研究和实际训练中的有效性。

Abstract: OpenLanguageModel (OLM) is an open-source PyTorch library for building and pretraining small language models while keeping their machinery visible. In OLM, model code reads like the architecture: components are ordinary modules, while Block, Residual, Repeat, and Parallel describe how they are wired. The resulting model can move unchanged from a teaching notebook to a complete pretraining run or a research ablation. OLM connects this readable model layer to tokenizers, local and streaming datasets, optimization, mixed precision, callbacks, checkpoints, and hardware-aware CPU, single-GPU, and single-node multi-GPU execution. We demonstrate the full path by tracing GPT-2 from diagram to code, launching a FineWeb-Edu training script, replacing one attention component, and letting AutoTrainer configure the available machine. The package includes 27 presets across nine familiar model families and documentation that progresses from LM fundamentals to architecture research. Validation shows close agreement with independent reference implementations, 90.6% four-GPU weak-scaling efficiency for a 348M-parameter workload, compact architecture edits, and positive early usability results. OLM is MIT-licensed and available through PyPI, GitHub, and its documentation site.

</details>


### [199] [SpecLA: Efficient Speculative Decoding for Linear-Attention Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16673)
*Zhibin Wang,Xuying Han,Zhaohua Yang,Fuliang Liu,Xue Li,Rong Gu,Sheng Zhong,Chen Tian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SpecLA，一种针对有状态线性注意力模型的投机解码运行时系统，通过拓扑感知验证、状态恢复和置信度剪枝等技术，在NVIDIA H100上实现最高1.70倍端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力模型用循环状态替代KV缓存，但自回归解码仍逐token操作效率低；现有投机解码系统专为Transformer设计，无法处理线性注意力模型的状态依赖、分支恢复和验证浪费问题。

Method: SpecLA包含三大技术：1) 拓扑感知核函数验证链和树结构；2) 验证过程中存储紧凑因子以恢复已接受状态；3) 置信度剪枝+目标对齐的EAGLE风格起草器生成高效候选。

Result: 在NVIDIA H100上以GDN-1.3B为目标模型，SpecLA相比自回归解码实现最高1.70倍端到端加速。

Conclusion: SpecLA有效解决了线性注意力模型在投机解码中面临的状态依赖、状态恢复和验证效率问题，显著提升了推理速度。

Abstract: Linear-attention models replace the growing KV cache with recurrent states, but autoregressive decoding still reads, updates, and writes these states one token at a time. Speculative decoding can reduce this cost by verifying several draft tokens in one target pass, yet existing speculative systems are designed for Transformer KV caches. For stateful linear-attention targets, verification must follow recurrent dependencies across chains and branches, acceptance must update only the accepted state trajectory, and the drafter must avoid submitting candidates that waste stateful verification work. This paper presents SpecLA, a speculative decoding runtime for stateful linear-attention models. SpecLA verifies chains and trees with topology-aware kernels, stores compact factors produced during verification to recover accepted states, and uses confidence pruning plus a target-aligned EAGLE-style drafter to feed useful candidates to the verifier. On an NVIDIA H100 with a public GDN-1.3B target, SpecLA achieves up to 1.70x end-to-end speedup over autoregressive decoding.

</details>


### [200] [Are Arithmetic Heuristic Neurons Form-Invariant? A Mechanistic Analysis of Symbols, Text, and Code in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16693)
*Sharath Naganna,Tanvir Ahmed Sijan,Uddipta Kalita*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究表明，大型语言模型中的算术计算在符号算术、自然语言问题和Python代码三种格式间具有形式不变性。共享的神经回路（一组稀疏的MLP神经元）跨格式存在，通过激活状态而非不同电路实现算术推理，跨格式错误主要源于激活状态差异而非独立电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型语言模型在不同等价表述下成功或失败的原因——是因为使用了不同的内部电路，还是共享电路的不同激活状态。具体关注算术推理是否具有跨格式（符号算术、自然语言问题、Python代码）的形式不变性。

Method: 使用结合归因修补（attribution patching）和激活修补（activation patching）的两阶段流水线，在三种Llama-3模型中识别算术启发式神经元。通过跨格式激活转移实验验证共享神经元的必要性和充分性。

Result: 发现一组紧凑的神经元在三种格式间共享，且该共享电路对后期层算术计算既必要又充分。跨格式转移共享神经元激活可恢复大部分错误预测（加减法超过97%），表明跨格式失败源于激活状态而非不同电路。

Conclusion: 大型语言模型的算术计算在神经元层面具有形式不变性，共享神经元在不同格式中属于相同的启发式家族，支持"启发式袋"（bag of heuristics）理论。

Abstract: Large language models often succeed on one formulation of a problem while failing on an equivalent formulation. Whether these failures arise from distinct internal circuits or different activation states of a shared circuit remains unknown. Recent mechanistic interpretability studies suggest that arithmetic in LLMs emerges from a "bag of heuristics," encoded by a sparse set of MLP neurons that represent distinct arithmetic strategies. We investigate whether arithmetic heuristic neurons are form-invariant across symbolic arithmetic, natural language word problems, and Python code in three Llama-3 models. In each format, we identify arithmetic heuristic neurons using a two-stage pipeline combining attribution patching and activation patching. A compact set of neurons is shared across all three formats, and targeted interventions show this shared circuit is both necessary and sufficient for late-layer arithmetic computation. Transferring the shared neurons' activations from a successful execution in one format to a failed execution in another recovers most incorrect predictions, exceeding 97% for addition and subtraction, indicating that cross-format failures arise from activation states rather than distinct circuits. Moreover, shared neurons consistently belong to the same heuristic families across formats, demonstrating that arithmetic computation in LLMs is largely form-invariant at the neuron level.

</details>


### [201] [Though Language Models Err While They Strive: Conformal Prediction for Self-Correcting Scientific Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16704)
*Mingqiao Mo,Yunlong Tan,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出科学可行性控制(SFC)框架，利用图结构共形预测对科学推理进行统计保证，通过渐进式绝对一致事实性验证，在物理推理基准上达到50.1%准确率，科学有效性91.7%，违规减少73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型生成技术内容时频繁违反基本科学原理，降低了其在科学应用中的可靠性。

Method: 提出SFC框架，将科学推理分解为原子级绝对一致事实性单元，构建近似可推导性图来建模逻辑依赖关系，通过实时验证和动态分支在检测到科学违规时切换到替代生成路径。

Result: 在PhyX物理推理上达到50.1%准确率，优于DeepSeek-R1(49.8%)和GPT-4(45.8%)，科学有效性91.7%（α=0.10置信水平），科学定律违规减少73%。

Conclusion: SFC框架通过图结构共形预测有效保证了科学推理的统计有效性，大幅减少了科学违规，为LLM在科学应用中的可靠性提供了新方案。

Abstract: Large language models frequently violate fundamental scientific principles when generating technical content, undermining their reliability in scientific applications. We introduce Scientific Feasibility Control SFC, a graph-structured conformal prediction framework that provides statistical guarantees for scientific reasoning validity through progressive absolute-coherent-factuality validation. Our approach decomposes scientific reasoning into atomic absolute-coherent-factuality units requiring both individual correctness against physical laws and logical substantiation from preceding context, addressing the cascade effect where early scientific errors contaminate subsequent reasoning steps. Unlike independence-based methods that treat claims in isolation, SFC models logical dependencies as approximate deducibility graphs and operates through real-time validation with dynamic branching when scientific violations are detected, the system branches to alternative generation paths using verified context as foundation. We demonstrate SFC across established scientific reasoning benchmarks including PhyX multimodal physics, MATH, ScienceQA, and ARC Challenge, achieving 50.1 percent accuracy on PhyX physics reasoning, substantially outperforming recent reasoning models including DeepSeek-R1 49.8 percent and GPT-4 45.8 percent while providing 91.7 percent scientific validity with formal conformal coverage guarantees at alpha equals 0.10 confidence level and reducing scientific law violations by 73 percent across multiple model architectures.

</details>


### [202] [JOR-Bench: Japanese Operations Research Benchmarks for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16777)
*Yuu Jinnai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: JOR-Bench是一个包含五个日文OR基准测试的数据集，涵盖1319个问题，用于评估LLM的运筹学建模能力。实验表明，强多语言模型在英日两种语言上的表现基本持平（平均准确率差异仅-0.3个百分点），但日文提示存在一些细微的跨语言差异，如模型有时会输出决策变量值而非目标值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏针对日语的运筹学（OR）问题求解基准，无法评估LLM在日语环境下建模和解决OR问题的能力。

Method: 将五个现有英文OR基准（IndustryOR、MAMO Complex LP、NL4OPT、OptiBench、OptMATH）翻译为日语，构建JOR-Bench数据集；使用OR-Tools的Python接口标准化执行流程；评估七个LLM在英日两种语言上的表现。

Result: 强多语言模型在英日两种语言上的平均准确率差异仅为-0.3个百分点；但错误分析发现，日文提示下模型在某些领域会出现语用歧义问题，输出决策变量值而非目标值。

Conclusion: OR建模能力对强多语言模型而言基本与语言无关，但存在细微的跨语言差异，尤其在语用歧义方面。

Abstract: We present JOR-Bench, a collection of five Japanese-language benchmarks for evaluating the ability of large language models (LLMs) to formulate and solve operations research (OR) problems. Each benchmark is a Japanese translation of an existing English benchmark: IndustryOR, MAMO Complex LP, NL4OPT, OptiBench, and OptMATH, covering 1,319 problems spanning linear programming, mixed-integer programming, non-linear programming, and combinatorial optimization. JOR-Bench is a solver-independent benchmark that can be used with any solver or programming language, and consists of pairs of Japanese problem statements and expected numerical answers. We evaluate seven LLMs, including multilingual general-purpose models and Japanese-specialized models, on both the original English and the new Japanese versions, and compare performance across languages. For the main evaluation, we standardize execution with the Python interface to OR-Tools to make model outputs comparable and reproducible with open-source software. Our results show that OR formulation ability is largely language-neutral for strong multilingual models; the overall average accuracy difference between English and Japanese is only $-0.3$ pp. Yet error analysis reveals subtle cross-lingual differences, including a pragmatic disambiguation failure in some domains that causes models to output decision-variable values instead of the objective value when the prompt is in Japanese.

</details>


### [203] [Cascading versus Joint Modeling for Hierarchical Offensive Language Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16790)
*Ruixing Ren,Junhui Zhao,Xiaoke Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验比较了层级分解与联合多任务两种范式在细粒度 offensive 语言检测中的表现，提出了三级级联检测系统，并量化了准确率与部署成本之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对层级分解与联合多任务两种范式在准确率、参数量和推理延迟方面的直接受控比较，也缺少对类别不平衡处理策略最优性的验证。

Method: 提出三级级联检测系统，每个子任务定制训练策略，并通过消融实验确定最优类别不平衡处理策略；同时训练联合多任务模型作为架构对照，在准确率、参数量和推理延迟三个维度进行实测比较。

Result: 级联系统在三个子任务上宏F1分别为0.795、0.716和0.557；消融实验表明凭直觉配置损失函数并非最优；级联管道中约五分之一错误源自第一阶段且无法被后续阶段纠正；相比联合多任务模型，级联架构在所有子任务上准确率更高，在最不平衡的子任务上宏F1提升7.1点，但参数量为3倍、推理延迟为1.67倍。

Conclusion: 建立了一个明确的量化权衡：级联架构的准确率优势需要以更高的部署成本（参数量和推理延迟）为代价。

Abstract: Fine-grained offensive language detection organizes labels into a hierarchical structure, for which two modeling paradigms exist: cascaded decomposition and joint multi-task modeling. Prior work rarely provides a direct, controlled comparison of the two paradigms in terms of accuracy, parameter count, and inference latency, and rarely verifies whether a chosen class-imbalance handling strategy is actually optimal. This paper proposes a three-level cascaded detection system whose training strategy is customized per subtask, together with two verification mechanisms. First, a controlled ablation study determines the best class-imbalance handling strategy for each subtask. Second, a joint multi-task model with a shared encoder is trained as an architectural control, yielding real measurements along the dimensions of accuracy, parameter count, and inference latency. Experiments show that the cascaded system attains macro-F1 scores of 0.795, 0.716, and 0.557 on the three subtasks of the official test set. The ablation study reveals that configuring the loss function purely by imbalance-severity intuition is suboptimal; reconfiguring based on the ablation results improves both performance and stability. End-to-end cascade evaluation shows that roughly one-fifth of the errors in the cascade pipeline originate from the first-stage filter and cannot be corrected by subsequent stages. Relative to the joint multi-task model, the cascaded architecture achieves higher accuracy on all three subtasks, with a 7.1-point macro-F1 gain on the most severely imbalanced subtask, at the cost of three times the parameters and 1.67 times the inference latency. Together, these results establish an explicit, quantifiable trade-off between the accuracy advantage of cascaded architectures and their deployment cost.

</details>


### [204] [Diagnosing Correctness Probes under Self-Judgement Confounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16799)
*Yi-Long Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现语言模型内部表征中，自我判断（SJ）信号比客观正确性（OC）信号更具跨任务可迁移性，表明仅凭可迁移性无法确认OC语义的真实存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究从语言模型隐藏状态中解码的正确性信号存在语义歧义：客观正确性（OC）与模型自我判断（SJ）高度一致，难以区分两者各自贡献。

Method: 构造OC与SJ冲突的实验场景，分别估计OC相关和SJ相关的表征方向，在数学推理和事实回忆任务中测试其跨领域可迁移性，并控制答案似然、序列长度等混淆因素。

Result: SJ相关方向在所有模型上均显著跨领域迁移；OC相关方向在所有条件下迁移效果低于随机基线。该迁移不对称性在模型中间至深层出现，并在MMLU和TruthfulQA上泛化。

Conclusion: 可迁移性不能作为判定表征包含客观正确性语义的证据；模型内部最可靠传递的是自我判断信号而非客观正确性信号。

Abstract: Hidden-state readouts can predict whether language-model outputs are correct, but objective correctness (OC) usually agrees with the model's own self-judgement (SJ), leaving the decoded signal semantically ambiguous. We construct conflict cases in which OC and SJ predict opposite readout orderings. On high-confidence disagreements, conventional correctness-labelled contrasts often rank incorrect/self-endorsed responses above correct/self-rejected responses, following SJ rather than OC. We estimate factorial SJ- and OC-associated directions and evaluate their polarity across mathematical reasoning and factual recall. Across four instruction-tuned models up to 14B parameters, the SJ-associated direction transfers above chance in both cross-domain directions for every model, whereas the OC-associated direction has a below-chance point estimate for the expected OC ordering in every corresponding condition. This transfer asymmetry develops across middle-to-late layers, persists under answer-likelihood, sequence-length, and null-direction controls, and extends to MMLU and binary TruthfulQA without target-domain direction fitting. Across the studied models and diagnostic subsets, the most reliably transferable component preserves SJ-associated polarity. Transferability alone therefore does not establish objective-correctness semantics.

</details>


### [205] [Schema-Constrained Document-Level Event Argument Extraction with Lightweight LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16808)
*Roberto Pietrantuono,Antonio Guerriero,Pouya Sattari*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究使用中等规模开源大语言模型（如Phi-4 14B）进行文档级事件论元抽取，通过角色集注入、LoRA微调和确定性解码后处理，在MAVEN-ARG基准上达到42.39% F1，超越GPT基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档级事件论元抽取面临长距离依赖、跨句语境和严格角色约束等挑战，导致边界错误、角色模糊和模式不一致问题，现有方法表现不佳。

Method: (1) 在提示中注入角色集以保证模式合规；(2) 使用LoRA进行参数高效的监督微调，推理和训练采用相同JSON接口；(3) 确定性解码后处理：验证JSON格式、过滤无效角色、去重论元、对齐文档窗口内的跨度。

Result: 微调的中等规模开源模型在提及、实体共指和事件共指三个评估层面均超越GPT基线，Phi-4 14B在事件共指层面达到42.39% F1。

Conclusion: 中等规模开源LLM通过合理的微调策略和后处理，可以可靠地进行文档级模式约束的EAE，超越更大的私有模型。

Abstract: Event Argument Extraction (EAE) converts documents into structured event records by identifying argument spans and assigning them schema-defined roles. Document-level EAE is challenging due to long-range dependencies between triggers and arguments, cross-sentence context, and strict role constraints, which often lead to boundary errors, uncertainty in roles, and inconsistencies with restricted schemas.
  In this paper, we study whether mid-sized open LLMs can perform schema-constrained EAE reliably at the document level on MAVEN-ARG. Our approach combines (i) role-set injection in prompts for schema compliance, (ii) parameter-efficient supervised fine-tuning (LoRA) using the same JSON-only interface used at inference, and (iii) deterministic decoding with post-processing that validates JSON, filters invalid roles, de-duplicates arguments, and aligns spans to the document window. Under the official MAVEN-ARG evaluator, fine-tuned mid-sized open models outperform previously reported GPT baselines across mention, entity-coreference, and event-coreference evaluations; our best model (Phi-4, 14B) reaches 42.39\% F1 at the event-coreference level. Code to reproduce experiments is publicly available at https://github.com/dessertlab/EAE/.

</details>


### [206] [Group Entropy-Controlled Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16850)
*Guangran Cheng,Chengqi Lyu,Songyang Gao,Wenwei Zhang,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GEPO提出了一种基于组熵的自适应优势塑形方法，通过缓解GRPO中不同任务熵分布不均导致的优势信号不可比问题，在多个基准测试上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO等强化学习方法在混合异构任务上使用全局或词元级熵调控，无法适应不同任务对探索-利用的差异化需求；此外，GRPO的标准化优势在不同提示组间存在熵相关偏差，导致优势信号统计上不可比。

Method: 提出GEPO方法，利用已有分组样本估计组熵，进行熵条件化的非对称优势塑形：对低熵组的正优势进行衰减以避免过度利用，对高熵组的负优势进行衰减以保留探索空间，并使用历史熵统计量推导自适应阈值。

Result: 在2个基础模型、13个基准（涵盖数学、物理、科学、代码生成和指令跟随）上的实验表明，GEPO持续优于GRPO及近期熵控制方法，实现跨任务均衡提升。

Conclusion: GEPO通过组级熵调控有效解决了异构任务下熵分布不均带来的优化问题，为LLM的强化学习对齐提供了更精细化的探索-利用平衡机制。

Abstract: Entropy control has become an effective tool in reinforcement learning (RL) of large language models (LLMs), helping balance exploration-exploitation trade-off during alignment process. Such RL paradigm is often conducted on mixtures of heterogeneous tasks, which induce distinct entropy regimes under the same policy, making global or token-level entropy regulation insufficient to corresponding heterogeneous needs of exploration. This heterogeneity further makes GRPO-style normalized advantages induce an entropy-dependent bias, making advantage signals across prompt groups statistically non-comparable. To address this issue, we propose Group Entropy-Controlled Policy Optimization (GEPO), a lightweight extension to GRPO that uses group entropy, estimated from existing grouped samples to perform entropy-conditioned asymmetric advantage shaping. GEPO attenuates positive advantages in low-entropy groups to reduce over-exploitation, and negative advantages in high-entropy groups to preserve exploration, with adaptive thresholds derived from historical entropy statistics. Extensive experiments on two base models across thirteen benchmarks spanning mathematics, physics, science, code generation, and instruction following show that GEPO consistently outperforms GRPO and recent entropy-controlled methods, delivering balanced cross-task improvements while preserving task-specific exploration levels throughout training.

</details>


### [207] [Trace-Based On-Policy Distillation for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16872)
*Haolin Ren,Ziyang Huang,Chenhao Yuan,Jun Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TOPD方法，通过教师监督的轨迹蒸馏，无需奖励建模即可将推理能力迁移至扩散大语言模型，在数学推理任务上以4倍更少的rollout轮次达到或超越强化学习训练的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLM）在后训练阶段面临挑战：监督微调需要密集但往往是离策略的掩码状态，而强化学习依赖稀疏奖励或价值建模。现有方法难以有效且高效地将推理能力注入dLLM。

Method: 提出TOPD（基于轨迹的在线策略蒸馏），核心是在目标dLLM自身的去噪轨迹上进行监督，聚焦于形成最终响应的轨迹对齐token决策。具体包括：从目标dLLM采样在线策略扩散轨迹，从教师模型获取对应部分去噪状态的token分布，使用token级别的反向KL散度更新目标dLLM。

Result: 在数学推理基准上，TOPD使SDAR-4B-Chat在MATH500准确率上匹配RL训练的TraDo-4B-Instruct，静态评估提升+5.7，动态评估提升+4.5。相比RL方法，TOPD仅需4×更少的rollout轮次，估计实现了96.0×的模型计算加速。

Conclusion: TOPD作为一种无需奖励估计的教师监督框架，能有效将推理能力迁移至dLLM，在保持密集教师监督的同时与模型自身去噪状态对齐训练，显著提升训练效率。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) are a promising alternative to autoregressive generation. However, reasoning-oriented post-training for dLLMs remains challenging. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) for dLLMs requires dense but often off-policy masked states, while reinforcement learning (RL) relies on sparse rewards or value modeling. This paper proposes \textbf{trace-based on-policy distillation (TOPD)}, a teacher-supervised framework that transfers reasoning ability to a target dLLM without reward estimation. The key idea is to supervise a dLLM on its own denoising trajectory, focusing on the trace-aligned token decisions that form the final response. Specifically, TOPD samples on-policy diffusion trajectories from the target dLLM, obtains teacher token distributions from a teacher model on the corresponding partially denoised states, and updates the target dLLM with a token-level Reverse Kullback-Leibler (Reverse-KL) objective. This design preserves dense teacher supervision while aligning training with the model's own denoising states. On mathematical reasoning benchmarks, TOPD enables SDAR-4B-Chat to match the MATH500 accuracy of its RL-trained counterpart TraDo-4B-Instruct, with gains of +5.7 under static evaluation and +4.5 under dynamic evaluation. Compared with the RL-trained counterpart, TOPD achieves this with 4$\times$ fewer rollout rounds, corresponding to an estimated 96.0$\times$ to-accuracy model-compute speedup.

</details>


### [208] [Real-World Evaluation of an AI Agent Drafting Translational Impact Summaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16989)
*Mohammad Arvan,Amber E. Osterholt,Bailee Rue,Yuvaneswaren Ramakrishnan Sureshbabu,Krishna Riteshkumar Patel,Rebecca T. Feinstein,Bethany C. Bray,Niranjan S. Karnik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种人在回路中的人工智能代理，用于自动化收集学者研究成果并生成TSBM影响摘要，将人工组装时间从15小时缩短至14分钟，且81.7%的发现被评审接受或编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CTSA项目需要记录学者的研究影响，但手动整理每位学者的资料耗时约15小时，无法扩展至整个队列，因此需要一种AI工具来自动化跨平台的数据收集。

Method: 构建了一个人在回路中（human-in-the-loop）的AI代理，自动收集学者的证据档案并起草TSBM影响摘要。在10名KL2/K12学者的工作流程中评估，两位评审独立编码507条发现为接受、编辑或拒绝，以一致可用率（双方接受或编辑的比例）为主要指标。

Result: 81.7%的发现被双方接受或编辑；每位评审员每名学者中位耗时14分钟（替代15小时人工）；Cohen's kappa为0.43（中等一致性）；AI的召回率接近人工搜索；影响证据覆盖全部四个TSBM领域；约三分之一的发现属于常规流程易遗漏的非学术类别；评审员对合成准确度评分为4.5/5，有用性为4.8/5。

Conclusion: 人在回路中的AI代理可作为学者影响记录的初稿撰写者，将工作人员从收集和撰写转变为评审，使队列规模的影响报告变得可行。

Abstract: Introduction. Clinical and Translational Science Award (CTSA) programs must document their scholars' research impact, but assembling each scholar's record by hand takes staff an estimated 15 hours and does not scale to a full cohort. An artificial intelligence (AI) agent could serve as a tool to gather scholar data across platforms and disciplines.
  Methods. We built a human-in-the-loop AI agent that assembles a dossier of sourced evidence for each scholar and drafts one-sentence Translational Science Benefits Model (TSBM) impact summaries for staff review. We evaluated it in the impact-reporting workflow of one CTSA hub across 10 career-development (KL2/K12) scholars. Two evaluation staff independently coded all 507 findings as accept, edit, or reject; the primary measure was the unanimous usable rate, defined as the share both accepted or edited.
  Results. Both reviewers accepted or edited 81.7% of the agent's findings. Reviewers each spent a median of 14 minutes per scholar, replacing an estimated 15 hours of manual assembly. Inter-rater agreement was moderate (Cohen's kappa 0.43 on the usable-versus-reject decision). A profile discovery study found the agent's recall close to human search. The agent's impact evidence spanned all four TSBM domains, and about a third of the reviewed findings fell in non-scholarly categories that routine processes tend to miss. Reviewers rated synthesis accuracy 4.5 and usefulness 4.8 on a 5-point scale.
  Conclusions. A human-in-the-loop AI agent can serve as the first-pass author of a scholar's impact record, shifting staff from collecting and writing to reviewing, and making cohort-scale impact reporting feasible.

</details>


### [209] [Learning from Synthetic Data without Model Collapse in Iterative Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17043)
*Xiaonan Luo,Yue Huang,Kehan Guo,Ping He,Chuan Zou,Ting Hua,Xiangliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出KITE框架解决大模型迭代训练中的模型坍塌问题，通过识别技能极化现象并针对性生成数据，实现比基线更稳定的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注如何约束模型坍塌的性能退化，但在迭代模型进化中，更有意义的目标是确保连续模型持续改进，需要更细粒度的诊断方法。

Method: 提出KITE（Knowledge-boundary Instruction Tuning via Exploration）两阶段框架：1）失败引导的数据生成；2）边界感知的不确定性筛选。

Result: 在多个数据集和开源LLM上的实验表明，KITE相比强合成数据基线实现了更稳定的改进。

Conclusion: 模型坍塌在指令微调中表现为技能极化（强化已有优势、削弱薄弱环节），KITE通过针对性数据生成可以有效缓解该问题。

Abstract: Model collapse is a central challenge in learning from synthetic data: as later-generation large language models (LLMs) are trained on an increasing proportion of model-generated data, performance can degrade due to narrowed coverage and accumulated bias. Existing work mainly studies how to bound this degradation. In iterative model evolution, however, the more meaningful objective is to ensure that each successive model improves over its predecessor, which requires diagnosing collapse at a granularity that is actionable for data curation. We study this problem in synthetic data self-improving for instruction tuning. We show that collapse in this setting is not simply uniform performance degradation, but can appear as a polarization of competence, where synthetic training reinforces already strong skills while further degrading weak ones. Motivated by this observation, we propose KITE (Knowledge-boundary Instruction Tuning via Exploration), a two-stage framework that combines failure-guided data generation with boundary-aware uncertainty curation. Experiments across several datasets and multiple open-source LLMs show that KITE yields more stable improvement than strong synthetic-data baselines.

</details>


### [210] [BLAD: A Historically Contextualized, Multilingual Dataset of Bangladeshi Legal Acts (1799 to 2025)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17111)
*Adib Sakhawat*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了孟加拉国法律法案数据集（BLAD），包含1799年至2025年间1,484部立法法案的全文、结构化章节、脚注、废止状态及元数据，支持对南亚低资源民法管辖区的时序与多语言法律NLP分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决南亚低资源、大陆法系司法管辖区在法律自然语言处理（NLP）资源方面长期存在的空白，为孟加拉国法律文本提供系统化、结构化的数据集。

Method: 通过采集和富集管道构建数据集，包含法案全文、结构化章节与脚注、废止状态、元数据（治理政权、国家元首、当时法律框架），涵盖英语、孟加拉语及混合语言文档。

Result: 成功构建了BLAD数据集，包含1,484部跨越1799-2025年的立法法案，提供了描述性统计，并公开数据集以供研究使用。

Conclusion: BLAD填补了南亚低资源法律NLP的数据空白，支持时序和多语言法律文本分析，并为未来研究方向提供了基础。

Abstract: We present the Bangladesh Legal Acts Dataset (BLAD), a curated collection of 1{,}484 legislative acts enacted between 1799 and 2025. Each act is represented with its full text, structured sections and footnotes, repeal status, and metadata linking it to the governing regime, head of state, and prevailing legal framework at the time of enactment. The corpus spans English, Bengali, and mixed-language documents, supporting temporal and multilingual analysis of statutory law. BLAD addresses a persistent gap in legal natural language processing (NLP) resources for low-resource, civil-law jurisdictions in South Asia. We describe the acquisition and enrichment pipeline, report descriptive statistics over more than two centuries of legislation, and outline the research directions the corpus enables. The dataset is publicly available under the CC~BY-SA~4.0 license at https://www.kaggle.com/datasets/sakhadib/bangladesh-legal-acts-dataset.

</details>


### [211] [Scope3Trace: Evidence-Based Identification and Extraction of Scope 3 GHG Emissions from Sustainability Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17122)
*Siyuan Zheng,Yifan Duan,Chao Xue,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Scope3Trace框架，用于从ESG报告中可解释、可追溯地提取范围3温室气体排放信息，结合文档信息提取管道、LLM辅助页面定位、表格重建以及混合规则-LLM提取与证据验证，实现高精度的组织级和建筑级排放披露提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 范围3 GHG排放占企业碳足迹的主要部分，但由于披露稀疏、报告格式异构以及证据可追溯性有限，难以大规模分析。现有方法缺乏明确的证据基础或依赖昂贵的人工标注和验证。

Method: 提出Scope3Trace框架，集成PDF收集与OCR解析、LLM辅助页面定位与表格重建、混合规则-LLM提取方法，并进行证据基础验证。同时构建了双层次、证据基础的多模态数据集。

Result: 在提取范围1-3总量及类别级披露方面达到了高精度，实现了异构可持续性披露的可靠提取与透明集成。

Conclusion: Scope3Trace能够有效解决范围3排放信息提取中的证据可追溯性和异构性问题，为大规模企业碳足迹分析提供了可靠的技术方案。

Abstract: Scope 3 greenhouse gas (GHG) emissions account for the majority of corporate carbon footprints, yet remain difficult to analyze at scale due to sparse disclosures, heterogeneous report document formats, and limited evidence traceability. Existing approaches typically rely on large language models to extract emissions information from ESG reports, but often lack explicit evidence grounding or depend on costly manual annotation and verification to ensure extraction reliability. To address these challenges, we propose Scope3Trace, an evidence-grounded information extraction framework designed to extract interpretable and traceable Scope 3 emissions information from real-world ESG and sustainability reports. The framework integrates a document information extraction pipeline that performs PDF collection and OCR parsing, LLM-assisted page localization and table reconstruction, and hybrid rule-LLM extraction of organization- and building-level emissions disclosures with evidence-grounded verification. Building upon this framework, we further contribute a dual-level, evidence-grounded, multimodal dataset comprising organization-level Scope 3 disclosures extracted from heterogeneous sustainability reports. Scope3Trace enables reliable extraction and transparent integration of heterogeneous sustainability disclosures, achieving high accuracy in extracting Scope 1-3 totals and category-level disclosures from sustainability reports.

</details>


### [212] [KyrgyzLLM-Bench: Benchmarking Kyrgyz Language Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17173)
*Timur Turatali,Aida Turdubaeva,Rustem Izmailov,Anton M. Alekseev,Sergey I. Nikolenko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估了26个大语言模型在吉尔吉斯语上的表现，发布了包含原生数据集和翻译后编辑数据集KyrgyzLLM-Bench基准，分析了跨语言迁移和翻译伪影对评估可靠性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言基准测试多依赖翻译后的英语数据集，难以反映目标语言的语言和文化特性，尤其对于吉尔吉斯语等低资源语言，缺乏可靠的原生评估数据。

Method: 构建KyrgyzLLM-Bench基准，包含KyrgyzMMLU和KyrgyzRC两个原生数据集，以及WinoGrande、HellaSwag、BoolQ、TruthfulQA的翻译后编辑版本；在零样本和少样本设置下评估26个开源和闭源LLM。

Result: 模型排名在WinoGrande和BoolQ上从英语到吉尔吉斯语大致可迁移，MMLU上迁移程度较低，HellaSwag存在显著的英-吉性能差距（源于翻译导致的合理性偏移）；少样本提示改进了部分开源模型的阅读理解能力，但对闭源模型的翻译任务表现不一致。

Conclusion: 公开了所有数据集、评估代码和结果，并将吉尔吉斯语任务集成到多语言评估框架中，以支持未来的吉尔吉斯语NLP研究。

Abstract: Evaluating large language models (LLMs) across languages remains challenging, as most multilingual benchmarks rely on translated English datasets, often obscuring linguistic and cultural specificity in the target language. This issue is particularly pronounced for less-resourced languages such as Kyrgyz, where reliable natively authored evaluation data are scarce. Building on previously introduced Kyrgyz-language evaluation datasets, this work reports the first systematic and large-scale evaluation of LLMs in Kyrgyz using the KyrgyzLLM-Bench benchmark suite. KyrgyzLLM-Bench comprises two natively authored datasets$-$KyrgyzMMLU and KyrgyzRC$-$together with carefully translated and manually post-edited versions of WinoGrande, HellaSwag, BoolQ, and TruthfulQA. We evaluate 26 open- and closed-source LLMs under zero-shot and few-shot settings, analyzing model performance, cross-lingual transfer, and the impact of translation artifacts on evaluation reliability. Across families and tasks, model rankings transfer broadly from English to Kyrgyz on WinoGrande and BoolQ, and to a lesser extent on MMLU, while HellaSwag exhibits a substantial English-Kyrgyz performance gap consistent with translation-induced plausibility shifts. Few-shot prompting improves several open-source models on reading comprehension but behaves inconsistently for proprietary models on translated tasks. We publicly release all datasets, evaluation code, and per-model results, and integrate the Kyrgyz tasks into a widely used multilingual evaluation framework to support future research on Kyrgyz NLP.

</details>


### [213] [Auditing Question-Order Effects in Large Language Models with the QQ Equality: Mechanism Characterization and a Saturation Caveat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17219)
*Pilsung Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文将量子问卷顺序效应中的QQ等式发展为审计大语言模型（LLM）顺序判断的准则，从理论、方法和实证三个层面进行了系统研究。理论上刻画了满足QQ等式的机制类别，方法上构建了预注册的审计管线，实证发现指令微调模型在强制二选一的next-token对数概率下无法通过分布级QQ审计，多数项目对呈现饱和状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类调查问卷中存在满足量子问卷顺序效应QQ等式的顺序效应，作者希望将其发展为审计大语言模型顺序判断一致性的准则，以检验LLM的响应是否具有类似人类的问卷顺序效应特征。

Method: 理论推导刻画了满足QQ等式的机制类别（边际独立核、极性依赖重复族等），方法上构建了包含最坏情况鲁棒包络、采样一致性抽查、完整标签平衡和饱和诊断的预注册审计管线，实证上对开放权重指令微调模型进行了两种框架下的强制二选一next-token对数概率审计。

Result: 所有预注册的健康门控通过，但18个项目中17个（框架一）和8个项目中7个（框架二）呈饱和状态（近乎确定性），没有项目被认证为残差情境性。强制二选一的next-token对数概率在测试条件下不足以进行分布级QQ审计。

Conclusion: 当将next-token分布视为调查响应分布时，应预注册饱和诊断。当前测试的LLM在强制二选一设定下无法满足QQ等式的审计要求。

Abstract: Human survey respondents exhibit question-order effects that satisfy the QQ (quantum question) equality, an a priori, parameter-free prediction of the projective quantum question-order model. We develop the QQ equality into an audit criterion for sequential judgments of autoregressive large language models (LLMs). Theoretically, we characterize which mechanism classes satisfy it robustly: marginal-independent kernels satisfy QQ iff all four mismatch transition rates coincide (a class containing the 2D rank-1 projective model with a fixed measurement pair under state variation); a polarity- and position-dependent repetition family is characterized by an exact cross-symmetry condition with closed-form violations; QQ-satisfying behaviors are closed under order-matched mixing; and the rank-2 Contextuality-by-Default criterion translates into audit coordinates as $|\qQQ|\le\OSS$, where $\OSS$ (the order-sensitivity score) totals the order sensitivity of the two marginals. Methodologically, we develop a pre-specified, audit-logged pipeline applicable to any model exposing next-token log-probabilities; it combines worst-case robustness envelopes, sampling-consistency spot checks, full label counterbalancing, and a saturation diagnostic. Empirically, in a first-signal pilot on an open-weight instruction-tuned model under two framings, all pre-specified health gates passed, yet 17/18 and 7/8 item pairs, respectively, were saturated (near-deterministic), and no item was certified residually contextual. Forced-binary next-token log-probabilities were thus inadequate for distribution-level QQ audits under the tested model and prompting conditions; we recommend pre-specified saturation diagnostics whenever next-token distributions are treated as survey-response distributions.

</details>


### [214] [Literary Non-Style in LLM-Generated Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17228)
*Cory Massaro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM生成文本在n-gram统计分布上存在一致模式，通过定性分析揭示了LLM风格的缺陷，并指出风格与语义问题不可分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究显示LLM文本在风格和语义范围上与人类存在差异，但缺乏对n-gram统计分布模式的系统分析。

Method: 统计LLM生成文本中n-gram的分布模式，并结合定性分析对n-gram进行风格缺陷评估。

Result: 发现了LLM生成文本中n-gram简单但一致的统计分布模式，高阶n-gram与语义内容相关。

Conclusion: 风格问题与语义问题无法截然分开，LLM在n-gram层面的统计模式反映了更深层次的风格与语义缺陷。

Abstract: Prior work on LLM-generated text has demonstrated quantitative and qualitative departures from text produced by humans. LLM-generated texts differ from human writing in style, resulting in a characteristic textual "feel," while the semantic range of LLMs is much restricted compared to that of humans. In this contribution, I note simple but consistent patterns in the statistical distribution of n-grams within LLM-generated text. Via qualitative analysis of these n-grams, I reveal deficiencies in LLM style. Because higher-order n-grams correlate to semantic content, I conclude that questions of style and semantics are not cleanly separable.

</details>


### [215] [Robust Summarization of Doctor-Patient Conversations: TalTech Systems for the Beyond Transcription Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17230)
*Aivo Olev,Tanel Alumäe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出在语音上直接生成SOAP笔记的方法，使用Voxtral模型结合LoRA微调和DAPO强化学习（以Open Medical Concept F1为奖励），在BeTraC挑战赛双赛道获得第一名，且幻觉率最低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常先转录音频再生成SOAP笔记，流程繁琐且可能丢失信息。作者希望探索直接从长对话录音生成SOAP笔记的路径，提升效率和准确性。

Method: 筛选开源语音大模型后，采用Voxtral Mini（轻量级）和Voxtral Small（重量级）进行LoRA监督微调，再使用DAPO强化学习，以挑战赛指标Open Medical Concept F1作为奖励函数进行优化。

Result: 在BeTraC挑战赛双赛道均获第一名，且独立LLM评估显示在所有参赛系统中幻觉率最低。

Conclusion: 强化学习以概念匹配指标为奖励不会牺牲事实可靠性；在文本转录上微调可迁移到语音输入，并提升对域外真实录音的鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper describes TalTech's submissions to the Beyond Transcription Challenge (BeTraC), which requires generating SOAP notes directly from long doctor-patient conversation recordings, without intermediate transcription. After screening open-weight speech LLMs for long-audio robustness, we adapted Voxtral Mini (lightweight track) and Voxtral Small (heavyweight track) with LoRA supervised fine-tuning followed by DAPO reinforcement learning that uses the challenge metric, Open Medical Concept F1, as its reward. Our systems ranked first in both tracks, and an independent LLM-as-a-judge evaluation showed the lowest hallucination rate among all submissions, indicating that reinforcement learning against a concept-matching metric need not compromise factual reliability. We also find that fine-tuning on text transcripts transfers well to speech input and appears to improve robustness on out-of-domain real recordings.

</details>


### [216] [AI_LectureNote: A Retrospective Pilot Study of a Post-ASR Workflow for English-Script Rendering and Semantic Drift in Korean-English Medical Lectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17237)
*Kyeongeon Lee,Donghoon Chang,Seungryeol Baek,Taehong Kim,Wonjun Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向韩英医学讲座的ASR后处理流程AI_LectureNote，可将语音转写文本改写为学习笔记，并恢复拉丁医学术语。初步评估显示英文术语还原率提升，但存在语义漂移和极性错误等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决韩英医学讲座中ASR输出文本不适合直接学习的问题，尤其是韩语语音转写将拉丁医学术语音译为韩文，导致学习者难以识别标准术语。

Method: 提出AI_LectureNote工作流，对Whisper-1和GPT-4o转写输出进行后处理，恢复拉丁医学术语并改写成学习笔记格式；通过4场讲座、5种条件进行回顾性评估。

Result: Whisper-1路径的英文脚本渲染率从0.39提升至0.71，GPT-4o路径从0.26提升至0.65；但后处理条件下分别出现34/282和36/282句语义漂移，以及11/101和13/101句极性失败。

Conclusion: 该流程能提升术语还原率，但语义忠实度尚不足；建议将表面准确性、术语渲染、分块一致性、医学含义保留作为独立评估维度。

Abstract: AI_LectureNote is a historical, readability-oriented post-ASR workflow for Korean-English medical lectures. It rewrites speech-to-text output into study transcripts while restoring Latin-script medical terms rather than Korean phonetic transliterations. We retrospectively evaluate the workflow on four author-recorded lectures across five conditions. In this pilot, post-processing raised the macro English-script rendering rate from 0.39 to 0.71 on the whisper-1 path and from 0.26 to 0.65 when applied to 3-minute chunked gpt-4o-transcribe output. However, English-script rendering did not imply semantic faithfulness: the two post-processed conditions showed semantic drift in 34 and 36 of 282 reference sentences and polarity failures in 11 and 13 of 101 polarity-cue rows. A descriptive cross-input comparison suggested different candidate failure patterns: polarity-failure sets overlapped more strongly across front-ends (Jaccard 0.60; 9 shared of 15 unioned failures) than general semantic-drift sets (Jaccard 0.23; 13 shared of 57 unioned drifts). This single-annotator pilot documents concrete failure modes rather than population rates and supports evaluating surface accuracy, term-script rendering, chunk-level script consistency, and medical-meaning preservation separately.

</details>


### [217] [EvolvingWorld: An Open-Schema Framework for Co-Evolving Role-Play Agents and World Model in Interactive Literary World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17250)
*Qing Zong,Yue Guo,Mengxin Yang,Yiwen Guo,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了EvolvingWorld框架，用于交互式文学世界中角色与世界的协同演化模拟，包含角色代理和世界模型两个模块，构建了基于57本书的大型数据集，实验证明能有效提升长期模拟中的角色与世界发展一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式文学模拟系统要么将角色视为静态模仿，要么孤立地生成场景，未能捕捉角色与世界随时间协同演化的动态过程。

Method: 提出EvolvingWorld框架，包含两个模块：角色代理（多角色扮演与持续角色演化）和基于LLM的世界模型（全局与局部状态维护、场景推进）。采用开放模式支持多样化文学世界，构建了7个可训练任务，从57本书中生成138,596个训练样本和222个测试快照。

Result: 实验表明EvolvingWorld能够有效提升长期模拟效果，维持角色与世界发展的持久性和连贯性。

Conclusion: EvolvingWorld通过角色与世界的协同演化机制，为交互式文学模拟提供了更真实、更一致的长期叙事生成方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces EvolvingWorld, a framework and benchmark for character and world co-evolution in interactive literary worlds. Existing systems either treat interactive literary simulation as static persona imitation or isolated scene generation, failing to capture how characters and worlds evolve together over time. To address this, EvolvingWorld models literary simulation as a long-horizon process where characters interact, scenes progress, and character and world states are persistently updated. Unlike prior systems relying on fixed schemas, EvolvingWorld adopts an open-schema framework to support simulation across diverse literary worlds. The framework consists of two coupled modules: a Character Agent for multi-character role-play and persistent profile evolution, and an LLM-based World Model for global and location/entity-level state maintenance and scene progression. Based on this architecture, we formulate 7 trainable tasks for scene initialization, interaction generation, and state update. We construct a dataset from 57 books, producing 138,596 supervised training samples and 222 snapshots for testing. Furthermore, we introduce a trajectory-level LLM-as-Judge evaluation protocol spanning 10 dimensions and 20 metrics. Experiments show that EvolvingWorld can improve long-horizon simulation by effectively maintaining persistent, coherent character and world development.

</details>


### [218] [Should Missing Modalities Always Be Necessary to Repair for Multi-modal Sentiment Analysis?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17262)
*Yubo Gao,Haotian Wu,Xiaoyu Xu,Yibo Yan,Hong Chen,Ruoshui Peng,Fei Pan,Puay Siew Tan,Zhuoran Gao,Yonghua Hei,Jie Zhang,Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SIEVE方法，挑战多模态情感分析中缺失模态必须修复的假设，通过样本级学习决策判断是否需要修复，实验表明该方法能显著提升现有修复方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法默认缺失模态需要修复，但研究发现完整模态并非总是最优，不同样本偏好不同的模态子集，因此需要样本级别的决策机制来判断是否应该修复。

Method: 提出SIEVE方法，包含直接预测分支和修复分支，通过样本级损失差异获取充分性信号，利用证据门控联合建模充分性和认知不确定性，作为即插即用模块可应用于任意修复方法。

Result: 在CMU-MOSI和IEMOCAP数据集上，SIEVE在多种缺失率下一致提升了代表性修复骨干网络的性能，接近双分支可达最优。

Conclusion: 缺失模态不一定需要修复，是否修复应是样本级别的可学习决策，SIEVE通过显式决策有效提升了多模态情感分析在缺失模态场景下的表现。

Abstract: Existing methods for multimodal sentiment analysis (MSA) under missing modalities usually follow a repair-first paradigm. We revisit this assumption and ask: \emph{should every missing modality be repaired?} A per-sample oracle analysis shows the answer is not always: full-modality input is optimal for only a small fraction of samples, and every modality subset is preferred by some samples. These results suggest that adding or repairing modalities may not always improve prediction, and that the utility of each modality is sample-dependent. Building on this finding, we propose \textbf{S}ufficiency-\textbf{I}nformed \textbf{E}vidential \textbf{V}al\textbf{vE} (\textbf{SIEVE}) that turns ``whether to repair'' into an explicit, learnable decision at the sample level. SIEVE compares a direct prediction branch with a repair branch, derives an empirical sufficiency signal from their per-sample loss gap, and routes each input through an evidential gate that jointly models sufficiency and its epistemic uncertainty. SIEVE is repair-agnostic: it operates as a plug-and-play decision on top of any explicit or implicit repair module, without modifying its internal design. Experiments on CMU-MOSI and IEMOCAP show that SIEVE consistently improves representative repair backbones across evaluated missing rates, and approaches the per-sample dual-branch achievable optimum.

</details>


### [219] [Debate-on-Graph: Reliable and Adaptive Reasoning of Large Language Model on Uncertain Knowledge Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17266)
*Peiji Yu,Xin Chen,Tianxing Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Debate-on-Graph (DoG)框架，利用不确定知识图谱（UKG）增强大语言模型（LLM）在问答任务中的推理能力。通过启发式搜索提取可靠子图，并引入多智能体辩论机制，实现自适应、可靠的推理，在四个基准数据集上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在问答任务中常出现幻觉和知识缺失问题，现有KG增强方法无法有效过滤知识图谱中的噪声和错误，反而可能放大幻觉。不确定知识图谱（UKG）通过置信度评分量化不确定性，为解决这一问题提供了新方向。

Method: 提出DoG框架：1）设计针对UKG的启发式搜索算法，提取可靠且与问题相关的子图，减少噪声和错误；2）引入多智能体辩论机制（Multi-Agent Debate），通过自适应对抗性辩论充分挖掘UKG中的知识，同时保持检索证据的可靠性。

Result: 在四个基准QA数据集上的实验表明，DoG在现有LLM推理方法和基于KG的基线方法中达到了最优性能，实现了可靠且自适应的推理。

Conclusion: DoG框架有效利用UKG中的不确定性信息，通过子图搜索和多智能体辩论协同增强LLM推理，显著提升了问答任务的可靠性和准确性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in natural language processing. However, LLMs often suffer from hallucinations and lack of relevant knowledge when dealing with question answering (QA) tasks. To mitigate these issues, knowledge graphs (KGs) have been utilized to enhance LLM reasoning. Nevertheless, KGs often contain noise and errors, while existing KG-enhanced LLM approaches are generally unable to identify and filter such noisy and erroneous content, which can instead amplify hallucinations and pose challenges for reliable reasoning. Uncertain knowledge graphs (UKGs), which associate each triple with a confidence score to quantify uncertainty, offer a promising direction to address this challenge. Compared with prior work, we investigate how to leverage UKGs to support LLMs for QA. We propose Debate-on-Graph (DoG), a new framework that enables LLMs and UKGs to collaborate adaptively for reliable reasoning. Specifically, we first design a heuristic search algorithm tailored for UKGs to extract reliable and question-relevant subgraphs, thereby reducing noise and errors in retrieved knowledge. We then introduce a Multi-Agent Debate mechanism, which yields reliable answers through adaptive adversarial debates, aiming to fully exploit the knowledge in UKGs while preserving the reliability of retrieved evidence. Extensive experiments on four benchmark QA datasets show that DoG achieves state-of-the-art performance over existing LLM reasoning methods and KG-based baselines, while enabling reliable and adaptive reasoning. Our code is available at https://github.com/seucoin/Debate-on-Graph.

</details>


### [220] [Safety That Does Not Transfer: Cross-Lingual Clinical Correctness Drift in Deployable Medical Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17270)
